"As with previous iterations of NGate, the malicious code allows the attackers to transfer NFC data from the victim's payment card to their own device and use it for contactless ATM cash-outs and unauthorized payments."  In addition, the malicious payload is capable of capturing the victim's payment card PIN and exfiltrating it to the threat actor's command-and-control (C2) server. The flaw, since patched, combines Antigravity's permitted file-creation capabilities with an insufficient input sanitization in Antigravity's native file-searching tool, find_by_name, to bypass the program's Strict Mode , a restrictive security configuration that limits network access, prevents out-of-workspace writes, and ensures all commands are being run within a sandbox context .
